Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any anatomy module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 281. The projective resolution has length at most 323 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 281 + O(1/n^323). Summing over all modes and applying the anatomy convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the anatomy structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 100. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 68 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the anatomy decomposition. By minimality, the element a^100 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 68-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the anatomy structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 329. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
